One more piece: the ρ (covering radius) exact computation for d ≤ 3, and the [½𝟙]-deepest-hole check. In the π-model: torus T = ℝ^d/ℤ^d (our model lattice = ℤ^d ✓): d(p) = min_{k∈ℤ^d} γ_{\bar K}(p − k); \bar K = Σ[−g_i, g_i] = 2Z − Σg_i: γ_{\bar K}(x) = min{r ≥ 0: x ∈ r\bar K} = min{r: x ∈ Σ[−r g_i, r g_i]} — computable via LP: minimize r s.t. x = Σ μ_i g_i with |μ_i| ≤ r: variables (μ, r): tiny LP — OR via the DFS-style: hmm — γ_{\bar K}(x) = min_r ... = max over facets... — PRACTICAL: γ_{\bar K}(x) = min over representations x = Σμ_ig_i of max|μ_i| — the "chebyshev-like" representation: solvable exactly: it's an LP: minimize R s.t.
